{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_vrtx_011Ch7CzGcKHu6tnkWKhbcNU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I fixed four separate defects in how sprite entities are drawn. One caveat first: the attached `recording.mp4` isn't in the container (`/app/.harbor_assets/` is empty), so I couldn't look at any frames. I found the bugs by reading the sprite rendering code and checking the geometry by hand. The client builds cleanly, but I haven't run the game to watch the scene, and this binary was built without unit tests, so none ran.\n\n1. **Billboard sprites were always mirrored** (`irr/src/CBillboardSceneNode.cpp`). The right and left edges of the quad had their texture coordinates swapped, so every `sprite`-visual entity showed its picture the wrong way round from every angle. I swapped them back.\n\n2. **Upright sprites were mirrored from behind** (`src/client/content_cao.cpp`, `addToScene`). The back face used the same corner positions as the front, just wound the other way, so the back face (second texture) appeared flipped. It now uses mirrored corner positions with normal winding, so the back reads correctly when you walk round it.\n\n3. **Camera-dependent frames picked the wrong picture once an entity was turned** (`updateTexturePos`). For sprites set to change frame with the viewing angle (`select_x_by_camera`), the code added the entity's yaw where it should subtract it. The front frame only came out right at yaw 0 or 180; at other headings, such as a walking penguin's, it showed the side frames swapped. It now subtracts, so the chosen frame depends only on where the camera is relative to the entity.\n\n4. **Carried sprites ignored their carrier's facing** (also `updateTexturePos`). A sprite attached to another object picked its frame from its own stored yaw, which nothing updates while it's attached. As the penguin turned, the sprite above its head kept choosing frames as if it faced a fixed direction. For attached sprites, the facing now comes from the full chain: the parent's rotation plus the attachment rotation. I also refresh that chain before working out the camera direction, so the frame isn't based on a stale position.\n\nFixes 1 and 2 restore what the original texture and vertex layouts should be. Fixes 3 and 4 hold however the entity is placed, turned or carried. Fix 4 is my reading of \"however it is carried\", not something I could confirm against the video."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":565,"cache_read_input_tokens":53008,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":565,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":10,"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1abd288f-639a-4581-bca1-2aa9302015ff","uuid":"423ea167-986f-4bce-9298-b41cce2acba6","timestamp":"2026-11-16T20:05:15.718Z"}
